-- [[ CLEANED FOR LOCAL EVAL ONLY ]]

-- [[ HTTP GUARD: block all non-Roblox network for personal eval ]]
do
  local function _safeHost(url)
    if type(url) ~= "string" then return false end
    local u = string.lower(url)
    if string.find(u, "roblox.com", 1, true) then return true end
    if string.find(u, "rbxcdn.com", 1, true) then return true end
    return false
  end
  local _httpGet = game.HttpGet
  local _httpGetAsync = game.HttpGetAsync
  game.HttpGet = function(self, url, ...)
    if not _safeHost(url) then
      error("[cleaned] blocked HttpGet: " .. tostring(url), 2)
    end
    return _httpGet(self, url, ...)
  end
  if _httpGetAsync then
    game.HttpGetAsync = function(self, url, ...)
      if not _safeHost(url) then
        error("[cleaned] blocked HttpGetAsync: " .. tostring(url), 2)
      end
      return _httpGetAsync(self, url, ...)
    end
  end
  local ok, http = pcall(function()
    return game:GetService("HttpService")
  end)
  if ok and http then
    local _req = http.RequestAsync
    if _req then
      http.RequestAsync = function(self, opts)
        local url = type(opts) == "table" and opts.Url or nil
        if not _safeHost(url) then
          return { Success = false, StatusCode = 0, StatusMessage = "blocked", Body = "" }
        end
        return _req(self, opts)
      end
    end
  end
  -- executor request APIs
  local function wrapReq(fn)
    if type(fn) ~= "function" then return fn end
    return function(opts)
      local url = type(opts) == "table" and (opts.Url or opts.url) or nil
      if not _safeHost(url) then
        return { Success = false, StatusCode = 0, Body = "", StatusMessage = "blocked" }
      end
      return fn(opts)
    end
  end
  if getgenv then
    local g = getgenv()
    if g.request then g.request = wrapReq(g.request) end
    if g.http_request then g.http_request = wrapReq(g.http_request) end
    if g.http and g.http.request then g.http.request = wrapReq(g.http.request) end
  end
  if syn and syn.request then syn.request = wrapReq(syn.request) end
  if fluxus and fluxus.request then fluxus.request = wrapReq(fluxus.request) end
  if request then request = wrapReq(request) end
end
-- [[ END HTTP GUARD ]]

  getfenv().ce_like_loadstring_fn = getfenv().ce_like_loadstring_fn or loadstring
  getfenv().l_fastload_enabled = true
  getfenv().is_from_loader = true
  if getgenv then
    getgenv().ce_like_loadstring_fn = getgenv().ce_like_loadstring_fn or loadstring
    getgenv().l_fastload_enabled = true
    getgenv().is_from_loader = true
  end
end)

	local function f542(p254)
		local version = p254.version
		if version == up0 then
			local t123 = { success = true, message = "" }
			return t123
		end
		if version == nil then
			local t124 = {
				success = false,
				message = string.format(
					"state version is missing; expected version %s; apply migrations first",
					tostring(up0)
				)
			}
			return t124
		end
		if type(version) ~= "number" then
			local t125 = {
				success = false,
				message = string.format(
					"state version has invalid type \"%s\"; expected number %s",
					tostring(type(version)),
					tostring(up0)
				)
			}
			return t125
		end
		if up0 < version then
			local t126 = {
				success = false,
				message = string.format(
					"state version %s is newer than supported version %s",
					tostring(version),
					tostring(up0)
				)
			}
			return t126
		end
		local t127 = {
			success = false,
			message = string.format(
				"state version %s is older than supported version %s; apply migrations first",
				tostring(version),
				tostring(up0)
			)
		}
		return t127
	end

local function f588()
	local function f589()
		if up0 == nil then
			return
		end
		local v1 = up0.autosave
		if type(v1) == "string" then
			up1:Set(true, true)
			up2:Set(up3(v1), true)

			up1:Set(false, true)
			up2:Set(nil, true)
		end
		local autoload = up0.autoload
		if type(autoload) == "string" then
			up4:Set(true, true)
			up5:Set(up3(autoload), true)
		else
			up4:Set(false, true)
			up5:Set(nil, true)
		end
		if not v1 then
			v1 = autoload
		end
		if type(v1) == "string" then
			up6:Set(up3(v1), true)
		end
	end

	end

	local function f598()
		local Value6 = up0.Value
		if up1 == nil or type(Value6) ~= "string" then
			return
		end
		up1:DeleteFile(Value6)
		if up2 ~= nil then
			if type(up2.autosave) == "string" and up3(up2.autosave) == Value6 then
				up2.autosave = nil
				up4:Set(false, true)
			end
			if type(up2.autoload) == "string" and up3(up2.autoload) == Value6 then
				up2.autoload = nil
				up5:Set(false, true)
			end
			if up6.Value == Value6 then
				up6:Set(nil, true)
			end
			if up7.Value == Value6 then
				up7:Set(nil, true)
			end
			up8:SaveState()
		end
		up9()
	end

	local function f1194(p262)

		if type(p262) ~= "string" then
			return nil
		end
		local v366 = up0(p262)
		if v366 == "" then
			return nil
		end
		local match = v366:match("^(%d+)$")
		if match == nil then
			return v366
		end
		return (string.format("rbxassetid://%s", tostring(match)))
	end

	local function f2249(p66, p67)

			end

				local v62 = math.clamp(p66._elapsedMs / p66._totalMs, 0, 1)
				v61 = (v61 + (Unit - p66._bakedEndDirection) * v62).Unit
			end

			up2(up3.SetRotation, up3, Vector2.new(v64, v63))
			if v60 then
				local Flickbot2 = up4.data.Flickbot

		function t29:_SampleTrajectory(p82)
			local _trajectory2 = self._trajectory
			self._elapsedMs = self._elapsedMs + p82 * 1000
			local v80 = self._trajectoryIndex

			end
			self._trajectoryIndex = v80
			local t37 = _trajectory2[v80]
			local t38 = _trajectory2[v80 + 1]
			if t38 == nil then
				return _trajectory2[#_trajectory2].direction, true
			end

			local v82 = 1
			if 0 < v81 then
				v82 = math.clamp((self._elapsedMs - t37.t) / v81, 0, 1)
			end
			return t37.direction:Lerp(t38.direction, v82), false
		end
